# Home page QA tracker (sections 6 → 12)

Car folder (only car source, read-only): `/Users/mac/Downloads/uk/bmw split/`

## Decisions (from the user, 2026-10-19)
- Section 12 light desktop = **Section 12.2.png**; 12.3 is a variant, not used.
- Section 12.1.png (1024px dark, different design) is an old draft: **ignored**.
- "SECTION N" labels stay **out** (AGENTS.md); their vertical space is kept.
- Phone refs are 2x screenshots: compare at **half the ref width** (≈431px).
- Dark-theme cars: a transparent copy may be made in `public/cars/home/` with **only the white backdrop removed** (source untouched).
- No mirroring without approval.

## Shared rules (taken from approved sections 1–5)
- Desktop container: `max-w-[1600px]`, side padding 75px at 1536 (sec5) / 5–5.7% (sec3–4).
- Section backgrounds: light `#fbfcfd`/`#fefefe`, dark `#060a10`/`#030c16`.
- Text: strong light `#0b1426`, soft light `#30374a`; dark strong `#fff`, soft `#c0c7d1`–`#d4d6db`.
- Brand blue `#1557d6` (accents/links), button blue `#0b47a8`/`#1452d4`, heading accent `.heading-accent`.
- Cards: radius 12px, light border `#e9ecf0` + shadow `0 4px 18px rgba(16,24,40,.06)`; dark `#07131f` / border `#16243a`.
- Desktop H2 54px/62px bold, tracking −0.02em; body 13–16px.
- Font: site-wide Arial (global). The refs use a geometric sans; not changed (would alter sections 1–5).
- No shared files are changed in this run unless noted below.

## Changes outside sections 6–12 (user-requested)
- 2026-10-19: Section 5 dark rear car. On request, the white floor patch left under the car by the background cut was removed. `public/cars/home/s5-rear-dark-clean.webp` is a cleaned copy (floor pixels turned into a soft dark shadow); `s5-rear-dark.webp` is kept. `homeSec5.json` `headerCars.rearDark` now points to the clean copy. No other Section 5 change.

## Mobile readability pass (2026-10-20, user request)
- Phone/tablet layouts of sections 6, 7, 9, 10 and 11 rebuilt as flowing, readable layouts (shared `src/components/home/mobileKit.js`): heading 32px, body 17px, entries 16–18px, labels 13px (raised again on request). Wide tables became stacked cards (no tiny text, no sideways scroll).
- Sections 1, 3 and 4: small phone labels raised (Section 4 rankings table became cards).
- Measured: smallest text on every section is 11px or more at 360 and 390px (was 4.1–9px). No horizontal overflow from 390 to 1920px.
- The phone layouts no longer copy the tiny type of the 2x reference captures; desktop layouts are unchanged.
